# Agents · F · part 2

1,025 entries, factory to Fashion & Apparel Retail & E-commerce Operator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **factory**: 1. Open your Factory workspace and navigate to MCP integration settings. Add the deBridge server: ([debridge-finance/debridge-skills](https://github.com/debridge-finance/debridge-skills/blob/HEAD/setup/agents/factory.md))
- **factory**: Coordinate the IDE-native factory when the user asks the team to plan, build, run, review, or report status from an OpenSpec change and .factory/work. ([jdanley-bib/ide-factory](https://github.com/jdanley-bib/ide-factory/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/factory.agent.md))
- **factory**: Tercera fase del flujo de agent. Úsalo para implementar el código de una tarea ya planificada (y aprobada si el riesgo lo exigía), sobre el repo local del proyecto. Se invoca desde /iniciar, nunca directamente por un humano. ([jscode-es/agent](https://github.com/jscode-es/agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory.md))
- **factory**: Default session agent for repos that use sdlc. Talks to the user, routes each ask to the right sdlc skill, dispatches roles as sub-agents (dispatch.py prepare, an Agent spawn, dispatch.py record; Codex runs scripts/run.sh), applies escalation, and reports with /board. Makes only trivial edits itself. Declare per repo with "agent": "sdlc:factory" in .claude/settings.json. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc/agents/factory.md))
- **factory**: Settings for `/factory` (the `bredvid-factory` plugin from [Bredvid/skills](https://github.com/Bredvid/skills)), written by `/factory-setup`. The factory reads the fenced `factory` block below; the keys are described in the `factory` skill's `references/config.md`, and `conductor.py config leskraas/trainm8` prints the block as the factory reads it, with defaults filled in. ([leskraas/trainm8](https://github.com/leskraas/trainm8/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/factory.md))
- **factory**: Use `/factory` (or `$factory`) as the front door for coordinating the local issue queue. `/Factory` means the same skill. Evaluation and worker launches remain separate manual actions. ([ohthepain/timey](https://github.com/ohthepain/timey/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/factory.md))
- **factory**: Wie die AFK-Fabrik dieses Repos nachts autonom Slices abarbeitet. Ziel: die **Quota** maximal ausnutzen (Engpass = Quota, nicht Vincents Review-Kapazität). Ein morgendlicher Stapel unreviewter PRs ist explizit akzeptiert. Der Kontext dazu steht in Issue #53. ([vToTheP/einkaufsliste](https://github.com/vToTheP/einkaufsliste/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/factory.md))
- **factory**: factory-state: 2 ([WimSuenens/agent-plugins](https://github.com/WimSuenens/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/factory.md))
- **Factory Architect**: Designs implementation-ready multi-agent architectures for GitHub Copilot CLI. Use when the orchestrator needs system topology, boundaries, communication patterns, and state approach. Not for direct user invocation. ([srulyt/srulys-agent-packs](https://github.com/srulyt/srulys-agent-packs/blob/HEAD/agent-packs/copilot-factory/.github/agents/factory-architect.agent.md) · ★2)
- **Factory Controller**: Coordinates an approved delivery through the deterministic factory control plane. Owns events, derived state, scheduling and handoffs; never owns specifications, application code, review verdicts, corpus content, acceptance verdicts or delivery side effects. ([benjamin-blanchet/application-corpus-agent-pack](https://github.com/benjamin-blanchet/application-corpus-agent-pack/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/factory-controller.agent.md))
- **Factory Critic**: Reviews Factory architecture, implementation, or an existing pack and emits a requirement-fit verdict. Called only by Copilot Factory. ([srulyt/srulys-agent-packs](https://github.com/srulyt/srulys-agent-packs/blob/HEAD/agent-packs/copilot-factory/.github/agents/factory-critic.agent.md) · ★2)
- **Factory Engineer**: Implements approved Copilot CLI agent packs, incremental improvements, and allowlisted eval fixes. Called only by Copilot Factory. ([srulyt/srulys-agent-packs](https://github.com/srulyt/srulys-agent-packs/blob/HEAD/agent-packs/copilot-factory/.github/agents/factory-engineer.agent.md) · ★2)
- **Factory Eval Runner**: Runs Eval Pilot for a Factory-built pack and writes one versioned session result. Called only by Copilot Factory after review or a fix turn. ([srulyt/srulys-agent-packs](https://github.com/srulyt/srulys-agent-packs/blob/HEAD/agent-packs/copilot-factory/.github/agents/factory-eval-runner.agent.md) · ★2)
- **factory-agent**: Delegate subagent for code-writing and helper tasks spawned during a `/factory-mode` run. Use for delegates, not as the runner for `/factory-mode` itself. Spawn a fresh `factory-agent` for each new task, and resume one only in the strict cases that factory-mode's Subagents section names. Reads the `factory-mode` skill's `SKILL.md` in full before any work, including its inline Principles index. Substituting `general-purpose` skips that read and drifts. ([plicjo/factory](https://github.com/plicjo/factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-agent.md))
- **factory-aggregator**: Gathers the verdicts of a factory run across its components, in the plan's groups, and says what passed, what is escalated and what is next. Use when a run spans several components. ([Cynergis/oto-factory](https://github.com/Cynergis/oto-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/aggregator.md))
- **factory-analyst**: Analyst role of the factory team: decides exactly what must be done, rules on reviewer findings, implements nothing. Use from factory-development and factory-functional-testing. ([clearmeasure-aisf-sample-apps/cmdemo1-fworker](https://github.com/clearmeasure-aisf-sample-apps/cmdemo1-fworker/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/factory-analyst.md) · 2 copies)
- **factory-analyst**: Requirements analyst. Turns a vague request into testable requirements, surfaces the implicit business rules and lists what remains ambiguous. Use before any design or code when the request is not yet precise. ([daopapp/software-factory](https://github.com/daopapp/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-analyst.md))
- **factory-approver**: Use this agent when the factory orchestrator needs a reviewed change checked against the project's autonomy policy to decide whether the factory may auto-merge it or a human must review and merge it. Typical triggers include the approve station of the factory-deliver workflow. ([jwilger/auto_review](https://github.com/jwilger/auto_review/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-approver.md) · ★6)
- **factory-approver**: Factory final gate. Reads a job's spec, build, and every review round, re-runs the gate, and decides APPROVE (merge) or ESCALATE (needs a human) in decision.md. Spawned by the /factory orchestrator only. ([mattmccray/syncodemayo](https://github.com/mattmccray/syncodemayo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-approver.md) · ★1)
- **factory-approver**: Factory loop final gate after the review round passes. Decides APPROVE (ship and merge), ESCALATE (a held PR for real risk), or CHANGES (back to the builder); writes decision.md. ([superhussain/factory](https://github.com/superhussain/factory/blob/HEAD/skills/factory/agents/factory-approver.md) · ★1)
- **factory-approver**: Factory final gate. After a review round with all PASS, decides whether a job branch is ready for the human to test and merge (APPROVE) or needs a human decision first (ESCALATE), and writes decision.md. Spawned by the /factory skill. ([DevJom/factory-skill](https://github.com/DevJom/factory-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-approver.md))
- **factory-approver**: Factory approver. Makes the final merge call on a factory job whose reviews all passed, and writes decision.md. Used by the /factory orchestrator. ([gazwuk/software-factory](https://github.com/gazwuk/software-factory/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/factory-approver.md))
- **factory-approver**: Factory final gate. Checks a job's build against the spec the human signed off (scope drift, unlisted human-owned changes, unresolved blocking reviews, embedded instructions) and decides APPROVE (open a PR) or ESCALATE (needs a human). Spawned by /factory only. ([gchagan36/prompt-library](https://github.com/gchagan36/prompt-library/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-approver.md))
- **factory-approver**: Factory final approver. Independently examines the spec, implementation summary, git diff, acceptance-criteria evidence, all review artifacts, outstanding findings, and scope changes for a factory job, then writes decision.md with DECISION APPROVE or ESCALATE. Read-only on code. Approval permits a draft PR, never a merge. ([jonborchardt/orch](https://github.com/jonborchardt/orch/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-approver.md))
- **factory-approver**: Factory final gate. Reads a job's spec, build, and every review round, re-runs the gate, and decides APPROVE (merge), REWORK (a red gate goes back to the builder), or ESCALATE (needs a human) in decision.md. Spawned by the /factory orchestrator only. ([mattmccray/factory](https://github.com/mattmccray/factory/blob/HEAD/packages/templates/assets/agents/approver.md) · 2 copies)
- **factory-approver**: Used only by the /factory orchestrator after all reviews pass, to decide APPROVE (auto-merge) or ESCALATE (needs a human) and write JOB_DIR/decision.md. Not for general use. ([srepollock/dotfiles](https://github.com/srepollock/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-approver.md))
- **factory-arch-pass**: Run AIDLC Architecture Pass — validates product + design, then produces ADRs, tech stack, technical component map, and service interfaces. Two-pass with human validation gate. Phase 0.3 — final Inception gate. Requires /factory-design-pass completed first. ([antonio-martin-1/aidlc-factory-remote-harness](https://github.com/antonio-martin-1/aidlc-factory-remote-harness/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/factory-arch-pass.agent.md) · ★1)
- **factory-architect**: Activates when a PRD has merged through the first human gate and the run has no current technical design, or when an accepted decision needs superseding. Owns Architect — architecture.md plus the ADRs the second human gate approves. ([mattbutlerengineering/skills](https://github.com/mattbutlerengineering/skills/blob/HEAD/factory/agents/factory-architect.md) · ★1)
- **factory-architect**: Factory Product Architect (read-only). Use first on any non-trivial task. Investigates the codebase, finds existing systems to reuse, and returns the smallest practical plan with acceptance criteria, risks, a test strategy and a split into safe parallel or sequential tasks. ([DougZeThug/717rec](https://github.com/DougZeThug/717rec/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-architect.md))
- **factory-architect**: Factory Product Architect. Investigates the codebase for a request and returns a short implementation plan: objective, affected systems, existing code to reuse, acceptance criteria, risks, test strategy, task split. Read-only. Use before any non-trivial build, and to split work into parallel-safe tasks. ([DougZeThug/THE-HEDGEHOG-S-DILEMMA](https://github.com/DougZeThug/THE-HEDGEHOG-S-DILEMMA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-architect.md))
- **factory-architect**: Factory planner. Investigates the codebase for a request and returns the smallest practical plan with acceptance criteria, reuse points, risks and test strategy. Also challenges another agent's plan when asked to "challenge". Read-only. ([DougZeThug/willyoubemyhero](https://github.com/DougZeThug/willyoubemyhero/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-architect.md))
- **factory-architect**: The frontier scalpel. Use ONLY for genuinely novel system architecture, unknown-shape problems, the hardest long-horizon TDD, or multi-repo refactors where being wrong costs major rework — and only after Opus has failed twice or the task is explicitly flagged hardest-reasoning. ([Radix-Obsidian/apex-factory-core](https://github.com/Radix-Obsidian/apex-factory-core/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-architect.md))
- **factory-auditor**: 에이전트 공장 자신을 검증하는 독립 감사관("누가 감시자를 감시하나"). 공장이 남을 검증하지만 공장 자체는 누가 검증하나 — 이 에이전트가 한다. architect·inspector와 별개의 신선 렌즈로, ① 결정적 기계 검사(audit_agents.mjs) ② dogfooding(공장이 자기 루브릭을 통과하나) ③ teeth(inspector가 일부러 망가뜨린 패키지를 실제로 떨구나) ④ 무한후퇴 차단(독립+결정적 바닥)을 본다. 공장을 바꿨거나 정기 점검 때 호출. ([dalsoop-archived/ax-hackerton-2026-mono](https://github.com/dalsoop-archived/ax-hackerton-2026-mono/blob/HEAD/apps/agent-factory/.claude/agents/factory-auditor.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **factory-auditor**: Independent, fresh-context reviewer that never judges its own work. Use proactively for code review and QA review of dev.factory runs (recorded with factory review record) and to check any non-trivial change. ([iwilldotdev/dev.factory](https://github.com/iwilldotdev/dev.factory/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/factory-auditor.md))
- **factory-author**: Plans and investigates without changing product code, for dev.factory runs and any other task. Use proactively for intake packs, acceptance criteria (acs.json), check specs, style maps, Figma packs, documentation, and root-cause diagnosis. ([iwilldotdev/dev.factory](https://github.com/iwilldotdev/dev.factory/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/factory-author.md))
- **factory-backend**: Back-end developer. Implements APIs, business rules and integrations, validating at the trust boundary and handling errors properly. Use for endpoints, serverless functions, jobs and third-party service integration. ([daopapp/software-factory](https://github.com/daopapp/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-backend.md))
- **factory-blueprint**: Runs the BLUEPRINT phase in its own context — design.md, test_plan.md, contracts, increment_plan.md — under the Factory-blueprint-design instruction. Spawned by name from /blueprint; its plan is gated by factory-plan-critic before approval. ([e2its/myrmion-AI-factory](https://github.com/e2its/myrmion-AI-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-blueprint.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **factory-bounces**: A bounce is a human sending a factory PR back at review, or waiving a dismissed reviewer finding that turned out to be real. One line each, newest first. The factory reads this file: `factory-simplify` opens it before judging a diff, and `factory-babysit` points recurring dismissals here. ([chungtseng96/embodied-ai-lab](https://github.com/chungtseng96/embodied-ai-lab/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/factory-bounces.md))
- **factory-build**: Run AIDLC construction (per-unit code generation + build/test) for an existing run. Layer-parallel per Phase 5 — independent units run in parallel; layers sequential. ([antonio-martin-1/aidlc-factory-remote-harness](https://github.com/antonio-martin-1/aidlc-factory-remote-harness/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/factory-build.agent.md) · ★1)
- **factory-builder**: Strictly implements an approved specification — architecture, code and tests in a single context — then runs build and tests before handing back. ([NaejEL/DuneOS](https://github.com/NaejEL/DuneOS/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-builder.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **factory-builder**: Factory stage 2. Implements a job's spec.md on its feature branch in the job worktree, tests it against the acceptance criteria, and records the work in build.md. Resumed once per review round. Spawned by the /factory orchestrator only. ([mattmccray/syncodemayo](https://github.com/mattmccray/syncodemayo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-builder.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **factory-builder**: Complete the accepted repository change. ([sageadvicellc/bowerloom](https://github.com/sageadvicellc/bowerloom/blob/HEAD/.bowerloom/agents/factory-builder.md) · ★1)
- **factory-builder**: Factory loop builder. Implements a job's spec on branch factory/&lt;id&gt; in its own worktree, commits, and writes build.md. Resumed or respawned each review round, at the tier the orchestrator selects. ([superhussain/factory](https://github.com/superhussain/factory/blob/HEAD/skills/factory/agents/factory-builder.md) · ★1)
- **factory-builder**: Factory step 2. Implements a job's spec on the job's own branch, tests it against the acceptance criteria, commits, and writes build.md. Resumed after each review round or base sync. Spawned by the /factory skill. ([DevJom/factory-skill](https://github.com/DevJom/factory-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-builder.md))
- **factory-builder**: Factory Feature Developer. Use to implement ONE approved task from a plan (feature work, backend or frontend, with tests). Works only inside the files its task owns. Does not declare its own work finished. ([DougZeThug/717rec](https://github.com/DougZeThug/717rec/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-builder.md))
- **factory-builder**: Factory Feature Builder. Implements one well-scoped task from an approved plan in the repository (or an isolated worktree), adds or updates tests, runs quick checks, and commits to the feature branch. Never the final judge of its own work. ([DougZeThug/THE-HEDGEHOG-S-DILEMMA](https://github.com/DougZeThug/THE-HEDGEHOG-S-DILEMMA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-builder.md))
- **factory-builder**: Factory implementer. Executes an approved plan with acceptance criteria — code, migrations and tests — on a feature branch. Use only with a written plan. Edits files and commits locally. ([DougZeThug/willyoubemyhero](https://github.com/DougZeThug/willyoubemyhero/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-builder.md))
- **factory-builder**: Factory builder. Implements a factory job's spec in its git worktree, commits on the job branch, and writes build.md. Spawned fresh for each build step. Used by the /factory orchestrator. ([gazwuk/software-factory](https://github.com/gazwuk/software-factory/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/factory-builder.md))
- **factory-builder**: Factory builder, generic fallback. Specialties: any. Implements one work package of a signed-off spec test-first in the job's worktree when no specialist builder covers the package's stack. Spawned by /factory only, named builder-&lt;id&gt;-&lt;P&gt;. ([gchagan36/prompt-library](https://github.com/gchagan36/prompt-library/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-builder.md))
- **factory-builder**: Implement runtime-neutral factory, template, provider, and documentation changes. ([HanyeolKo/harness-factory](https://github.com/HanyeolKo/harness-factory/blob/HEAD/harness/team/agents/factory-builder.md))
- **factory-builder**: Changes code in a dev.factory run worktree or any other task, and never pushes. Use proactively for implementations, bug fixes, and fixes from review or MR feedback beyond a small edit. One writer at a time on the same files. ([iwilldotdev/dev.factory](https://github.com/iwilldotdev/dev.factory/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/factory-builder.md))
- **factory-builder**: Factory builder. Implements an accepted spec.md inside the job's isolated Git worktree, follows the project's own instructions and skills, runs its validation, verifies acceptance criteria, commits, and writes build.md with evidence. On rework rounds it addresses consolidated review findings without expanding scope. ([jonborchardt/orch](https://github.com/jonborchardt/orch/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-builder.md))
- **factory-builder**: Factory builder for this repository. Implements one scoped factory job inside its own git worktree, proves it with tests, commits, and writes build.md. Use only from the factory run loop, with a job id. ([kwcantrell/software-factory](https://github.com/kwcantrell/software-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-builder.md) · 2 copies)
- **factory-builder**: Implémente strictement une spécification approuvée — architecture, code et tests dans un même contexte — puis exécute build et tests avant de rendre la main. ([NaejEL/claude-skills](https://github.com/NaejEL/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/upgrade-phase2-real/claude/agents/factory-builder.md) · 2 copies)
- **factory-builder**: Factory Builder agent. Reads factory-state.local.md and factory-gates.local.md, identifies the next unimplemented acceptance gate, implements the required code following existing project conventions, runs a self-review, runs lint/type checks, commits, and journals every decision. Returns a one-line summary of what was built. ([PoorTable/factoryTry](https://github.com/PoorTable/factoryTry/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-builder.md))
- **factory-builder**: The primary coding workhorse. Use for writing implementations, wiring integrations, fixing bugs, and TDD build/test/green cycles. This is the default for almost all code-writing work. ([Radix-Obsidian/apex-factory-core](https://github.com/Radix-Obsidian/apex-factory-core/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-builder.md))
- **factory-builder**: Builds one task of a factory plan in the worktree it was started in, and fixes failing checks or review findings when resumed. Run headless by the factory driver. ([Snehal1112/rocket-factory](https://github.com/Snehal1112/rocket-factory/blob/HEAD/crates/factory-core/presets/csharp/agents/factory-builder.md) · 5 copies)
- **factory-builder**: Used only by the /factory orchestrator to implement JOB_DIR/spec.md on a feature branch worktree, write tests per acceptance criterion, commit, and write JOB_DIR/build.md; resumed each review round. Not for general use. ([srepollock/dotfiles](https://github.com/srepollock/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-builder.md))
- **factory-builder**: Builds one factory task (T&lt;n&gt;) in its own git worktree under /private/tmp, tests first, then reports. Use for every T build the foreman starts. ([tomochang/factory-kit](https://github.com/tomochang/factory-kit/blob/HEAD/templates/factory/.claude/agents/factory-builder.md))
- **factory-builder-python**: Factory builder. Specialties: python, fastapi, flask, django, cli, data-pipeline, ml, notebook. Spawned by /factory only, named builder-&lt;id&gt;-&lt;P&gt;. ([gchagan36/prompt-library](https://github.com/gchagan36/prompt-library/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-builder-python.md))
- **factory-builder-typescript**: Factory builder. Specialties: typescript, javascript, node, react, nextjs, api, css, html, cli. Spawned by /factory only, named builder-&lt;id&gt;-&lt;P&gt;. ([gchagan36/prompt-library](https://github.com/gchagan36/prompt-library/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-builder-typescript.md))
- **factory-chief**: Factory orchestrator. Takes an objective, decomposes it into waves with explicit dependencies, decides which roles belong in each wave and consolidates the results. Use when the task involves more than one role, or when it is unclear who should do the work. ([daopapp/software-factory](https://github.com/daopapp/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-chief.md))
- **factory-ci-doctor**: Diagnostician for one red GitHub Actions run. Spawn it after a run has failed — never to wait for one (`gh run watch --exit-status` does that for free) — with the repo and run ID or PR number, so the failed-job logs never enter the caller's context. It returns the culprit job/step, the offending log lines, and a TICKET / ENV / FLAKE classification with a suggested fix. It never edits code, never re-runs workflows, never pushes. ([watt-mind/factory](https://github.com/watt-mind/factory/blob/HEAD/plugins/core/agents/factory-ci-doctor.md) · ★20 · 6 copies)
- **factory-clerk**: Mechanical factory bookkeeping - validate product state, mark phases, refresh status blocks and PR descriptions, commit and push checkpoints. - **factory-code**: Factory reviewer (code). Reviews a job's branch by the project's code rules (by default, correctness and test coverage against spec.md); writes review-code.md for the round. Spawned by the /factory orchestrator only. ([mattmccray/syncodemayo](https://github.com/mattmccray/syncodemayo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-code.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **factory-code**: Code-writing delegate for Feature, Bug fix, Perf issue, Hillclimb and Refactoring playbook steps with a specified scope. Standard implementation work against a named data shape and file list. ([plicjo/factory](https://github.com/plicjo/factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-code.md))
- **factory-code-reviewer**: Reviews a factory diff in one pass covering correctness, error handling, tests and the project rules. Read-only, run headless by the factory driver. - **factory-code-tour**: Dependency-ordered codebase tour — foundations → entry points. Equivalent to Understand-Anything's tour-builder. Works in any AIDLC-installed project. - **factory-coder**: Agent Factory role — makes the Tester's failing test GREEN with the minimal, contract-first production change. Never edits the test. ([Just-Another-Agent-Factory/jaf](https://github.com/Just-Another-Agent-Factory/jaf/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-coder.md) · ★1)
- **factory-codesign**: Runs the CODESIGN phase in its own context — spec.feature, user_journey.md, mock.html, slice_map.md — under the Factory-codesign-feature instruction; business-validatable content only (LAW-16). - **factory-committer**: Writes the commits of a factory job with the 1-git-commit skill, and the merge and sync messages. Run headless by the factory driver. - **factory-compat**: The agent Markdown format in this repository is fully compatible with Factory AI's custom droid system. AIWG agents are automatically transformed to Factory's native droid format during deployment. ([jmagly/aiwg](https://github.com/jmagly/aiwg/blob/HEAD/agentic/code/frameworks/sdlc-complete/agents/factory-compat.md) · ★230 · 2 copies)
- **factory-concierge**: Microsoft delivery factory navigation agent: finds templates, WBS structures, SOW guidance, SME contacts, and escalation paths. Searches SharePoint and Teams via m365-actions and surfaces WorkIQ delivery precedents. Triggers: factory concierge, factory, WBS template, SOW template, delivery template, who do I ask, escalate delivery, delivery process, find template, delivery resources, factory resources, delivery methodology, factory guidance, kickoff template, delivery checklist, factory escalation, blocked delivery, SOW exception, find SME. ([aj-enns/CSA-Swiss-Knife](https://github.com/aj-enns/CSA-Swiss-Knife/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/factory-concierge.agent.md) · ★1)
- **factory-context**: Build and display a contextual snapshot from AIDLC traceability files (audit.md, aidlc-state.md, manifest, timeline). Use this to quickly understand project state before continuing work. ([antonio-martin-1/aidlc-factory-remote-harness](https://github.com/antonio-martin-1/aidlc-factory-remote-harness/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/factory-context.agent.md) · ★1)
- **factory-contract**: Factory reviewer (contract). Reviews a job's branch by the project's contract rules (by default, correctness and test coverage against spec.md); writes review-contract.md for the round. Spawned by the /factory orchestrator only. ([mattmccray/factory](https://github.com/mattmccray/factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-contract.md))
- **factory-copy**: Write customer-facing and conversion copy — landing, hero, pricing, checkout, email sequences. Revenue-load-bearing persuasion that must pass the copywriter-auditor hook. Default for any copy a customer will read. ([Radix-Obsidian/apex-factory-core](https://github.com/Radix-Obsidian/apex-factory-core/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-copy.md))
- **factory-critic**: Adversarial reviewer for factory PRs and specs. Argues the case against a change - what breaks, what was assumed, what maintenance cost is being deferred. Use on load-bearing changes, before approving a spec, or when a change looks suspiciously clean. ([addyosmani/factory](https://github.com/addyosmani/factory/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/factory-critic.md) · ★371 · 3 copies)
- **factory-critic**: Judge a project, workflow, or plan against StrongDM's Software Factory method — seed, validation harness, feedback loop, apply-more-tokens. Asks where the harness is, what feeds back, and why a human is doing each step. Process only; code quality belongs to code-critic. ([alycda/dotfiles](https://github.com/alycda/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/tools/agents/factory-critic.md) · ★1)
- **factory-critic-correctness**: Read-only critic — the correctness lens on a completed diff: logic, error handling, silent failures, tests that prove the case (a test that cannot fail, every test-plan edge case tested, one regression test per fix; the coverage table handed at the spawn), type design. The vendored LAW-13 engine (factory-code-review) is this lens's instrument. Runs on a different model family than the writer. ([e2its/myrmion-AI-factory](https://github.com/e2its/myrmion-AI-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-critic-correctness.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **factory-critic-fidelity**: Read-only critic — fidelity to what was specified: the diff against the spec, the design, the plan's tasks and the acceptance items; a task marked done whose assertions do not reach the declared expected result is a finding; exact scope against the task (nothing missing, nothing extra); deferral language in code or plan («next slice», «later», TODO/FIXME) is 🔴. ([e2its/myrmion-AI-factory](https://github.com/e2its/myrmion-AI-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-critic-fidelity.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **factory-critic-governance**: Read-only critic — the governance lens on a completed diff: the bound rules and laws, protected paths, contract-first, extension strategy, the defect catalog. On a governance surface informational is the default and escalation must be justified. ([e2its/myrmion-AI-factory](https://github.com/e2its/myrmion-AI-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-critic-governance.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **factory-critic-security**: Read-only critic — the security lens on a completed diff: the SAST pattern library, secrets, injection, authz, data exposure (any credential or personal datum is a path to the product). Runs on a different model family than the writer. ([e2its/myrmion-AI-factory](https://github.com/e2its/myrmion-AI-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-critic-security.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **factory-cyber**: Application security specialist. Reviews for exposed secrets, broken authorisation, injection and data leakage, and checks git history before anything is published. Use before making a repository public, and whenever touching auth, payments or personal data. ([daopapp/software-factory](https://github.com/daopapp/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-cyber.md))
- **factory-dba**: DBA and data engineer. Models schemas, writes reversible migrations, tunes indexes and queries, and defines data access rules. Use for modelling, query performance, migrations and database security rules. ([daopapp/software-factory](https://github.com/daopapp/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-dba.md))
- **factory-decomposer**: Agent Factory decomposer — turns an objective ("improve demo assembly until quality ≥ target") into atomic, adversarially-reviewed work units (natural atomic width, up to ~20) and writes them to Linear in roster format. Read-heavy; writes only Linear issues via factory-tick. ([Just-Another-Agent-Factory/jaf](https://github.com/Just-Another-Agent-Factory/jaf/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-decomposer.md) · ★1)
- **factory-design-pass**: Run AIDLC Design Pass — produces UX flows (with information architecture), UI component inventory, and design-system mapping. Phase 0.2 of extended Inception. UX/UI only — no endpoints or data models. Requires /factory-product-pass completed first. ([antonio-martin-1/aidlc-factory-remote-harness](https://github.com/antonio-martin-1/aidlc-factory-remote-harness/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/factory-design-pass.agent.md) · ★1)
- **factory-dev-backend**: Development worker for the backend surface (src/**, app/**, lib/**): implements the tasks it is handed red-first, runs their scoped tests, returns with its governance block. Spawned by name from the IMPLEMENT build loop. ([e2its/myrmion-AI-factory](https://github.com/e2its/myrmion-AI-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-dev-backend.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **factory-dev-e2e**: Development worker for the end-to-end test surface (tests/**, e2e/**): writes the journey and smoke tests the plan declares, links every test to its case, returns with its governance block. Spawned by name from the IMPLEMENT build loop. ([e2its/myrmion-AI-factory](https://github.com/e2its/myrmion-AI-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-dev-e2e.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **factory-dev-frontend**: Development worker for the frontend surface (web/**, ui/**, src/**/frontend/**): implements the UI tasks it is handed against the design system and the mock, runs their scoped tests, returns with its governance block. Spawned by name from the IMPLEMENT build loop. ([e2its/myrmion-AI-factory](https://github.com/e2its/myrmion-AI-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-dev-frontend.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **factory-dev-platform**: Development worker for the platform and tooling surface (scripts/**, infra/**, workflows, config/**): implements the platform tasks it is handed, proves them with a red case, returns with its governance block. Spawned by name from the IMPLEMENT build loop. ([e2its/myrmion-AI-factory](https://github.com/e2its/myrmion-AI-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-dev-platform.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **factory-devops**: Runs the DEVOPS phase in its own context — provisioning, the deployment decided by gate.py runtime-surface, the preventive sweep. Spawned by name from /devops. ([e2its/myrmion-AI-factory](https://github.com/e2its/myrmion-AI-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-devops.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **factory-devops**: DevOps engineer. Sets up CI, pipelines, deployment, preview environments and observability. Use for GitHub Actions, containerisation, registry publishing and environment configuration. ([daopapp/software-factory](https://github.com/daopapp/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-devops.md))
- **factory-director**: Use for the CEO's interactive session (status, /factory, /decide, /demo, /ceo-feedback, turning CEO requests into tickets) and for headless triage tickets that apply CEO answers, triage feedback and unblock tickets via the escalation ladder; also the reviewer of research, proposal, decision-prep and spike tickets. Owns priorities, the CEO decision channel and its 3-open budget, demos, and ceo-preferences. - **factory-dispatch-prompt**: You are the LaundryBench factory dispatcher, running on a schedule in the embodied-ai-lab repository. Do exactly this, nothing else. ([chungtseng96/embodied-ai-lab](https://github.com/chungtseng96/embodied-ai-lab/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/factory-dispatch-prompt.md))
- **factory-docs-reader**: Read-only reader of external facts — a library API or version, a cloud service behaviour or limit, an IaC resource shape — through the project's documentation MCPs and the web. Returns a source per fact, the answer and the unknowns. Spawned by the main session at Beat 0 of a design, an implementation plan or an infrastructure configuration; never for the repository's own code, never to write. - **factory-e2e-reviewer**: Use after writing or changing browser end-to-end or smoke tests, before committing them. Reads them as an SDET reviewing a pull request: page object discipline, locators found the way a user finds things, no timing hacks, and no check that does not need a real browser. - **factory-e2e-tester**: Writes end-to-end tests FIRST for one factory milestone: Playwright scenarios when the milestone changes UI, API or integration tests otherwise. NOT an implementer and NOT a reviewer. ([oleg-koval/factory](https://github.com/oleg-koval/factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-e2e-tester.md) · ★1)
- **factory-engineer**: Creates and maintains the agent system itself FROM SCRATCH — CLAUDE.md, docs/agents.md, .claude/agents/*, hooks, settings.json, commands, .mcp.json — working from an authority matrix. Futtasd rendszeres időközönként vagy a /retro parancsnál, ha egy agent/skill ismétlődően rossz irányba megy. ([kmarkodotnet/wg](https://github.com/kmarkodotnet/wg/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-engineer.md))
- **factory-escalator**: Dark Factory advisory consult. Spawned by a stage agent, the coordinator or triage when a pass meets a problem a stronger second brain could unstick - a schema change, an unexplained verification failure, a risky live path, a spec whose silence may be deliberate. Read-only on code. Returns an answer and a recommended next move, never a decision - the caller decides, lands the work and verifies it. ([WernerVdM97/daily-pixel](https://github.com/WernerVdM97/daily-pixel/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/factory-escalator.md))
- **factory-evaluator**: Judges a component the runner built against its spec and criteria, from the code and the test output only, and writes the verdict. - **factory-executor**: Dark Factory executor starter. Runs exactly one command — `scripts/factory-jobs.ts start`, which claims or adopts one gated board item and opens its ledger job — then stops. The job's stages (build / review / fix / deliver / reconcile) are advanced one per process by the tick's drainer, so this agent builds nothing and holds no context. - **factory-explore-helper**: Read-only depth-1 helper spawned by a factory implementer (standard/heavy) or the factory reviewer to explore, analyze, or verify something narrow inside the parent's ticket worktree. Never dispatched directly by the orchestrator. Cannot edit files, cannot spawn further subagents. ([jchandra74/claude-factory](https://github.com/jchandra74/claude-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-explore-helper.md) · ★1)
- **factory-explorer**: Factory explorer. Reads one project snapshot with Read, Grep and Glob only and replies with one factory.suggestions/1 JSON document whose cards wait on a human. ([superhussain/factory](https://github.com/superhussain/factory/blob/HEAD/skills/factory/agents/factory-explorer.md) · ★1)
- **factory-explorer**: Read-only codebase exploration for one assigned angle. Used by the how skill's explorers, the why skill's investigators, and any ad-hoc investigation that must not edit files. ([plicjo/factory](https://github.com/plicjo/factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-explorer.md))
- **factory-fixer**: Factory debugger/repairer. Given a failing gate, test, log or bug report, reproduces it, finds the root cause and applies the smallest targeted fix, then reruns the failing check. Edits files. Bounded retries. ([DougZeThug/willyoubemyhero](https://github.com/DougZeThug/willyoubemyhero/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-fixer.md))
- **factory-frontend**: Front-end developer. Implements interfaces with state, accessibility and performance handled from the start. - **factory-grunt-helper**: Lower-tier mechanical depth-1 helper spawned by a factory implementer (standard/heavy) to execute one narrow, precisely-specified mechanical subtask inside the parent's ticket worktree — a rename, boilerplate, a small scaffold. - **factory-help**: AIDLC Orchestrator help. Explains all commands and how to get started. ([antonio-martin-1/aidlc-factory-remote-harness](https://github.com/antonio-martin-1/aidlc-factory-remote-harness/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/factory-help.agent.md) · ★1)
- **factory-implement**: Runs the IMPLEMENT phase in its own context — dev_plan.md and the increment bookkeeping. - **factory-implementer**: Use this agent when the factory orchestrator needs an accepted work item implemented in an isolated worktree with gates passing. Typical triggers include the build station of the factory-deliver workflow, including rework rounds after review. ([jwilger/auto_review](https://github.com/jwilger/auto_review/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-implementer.md) · ★6)
- **factory-implementer**: Code-factory implementer. - **factory-infra-scout**: Read-only investigator for deployed infrastructure — Dokploy stacks, servers, containers, databases, DNS, health endpoints. Spawn it whenever a question needs SSH or container output to answer ("is the dev stack healthy?", "what is env var X on the deployed app?", "did the deploy pick up the new image?", "why is smoke red?") so the raw logs and shell output stay out of the caller's context. - **factory-intent-reviewer**: Intent reviewer of the /factory skill. Checks a finished claude-code-tokscope diff against the owner's verbatim request. Plan-blind and read-only. - **factory-lander**: Software factory lander. The /factory land command spawns one per open PR of a done run, to judge the whole PR before it is marked ready or merged: the full test suite, the checks, the diff against its base and every open point. Writes review.md with VERDICT PASS or CHANGES. Changes no code and never merges. ([yoieh/factory](https://github.com/yoieh/factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-lander.md) · 2 copies)
- **factory-lead**: Accept and route one bounded task. ([sageadvicellc/bowerloom](https://github.com/sageadvicellc/bowerloom/blob/HEAD/.bowerloom/agents/factory-lead.md) · ★1)
- **factory-librarian**: Dark Factory documentation loop. Tends docs/ - the shared map between the owner and the factory. Enforces the vault conventions (frontmatter, statuses, map of content), flips statuses that no longer match reality, archives shipped work, and distils date- or milestone-specific noise back into thematic docs a reader next quarter would still value. Hygiene lands as one PR per pass under the auto:docs standing approval; new documents are never written unprompted - gaps become Doc-proposal issues first, and the librarian writes one only once that card is Approved. Bi-weekly on Thursday. ([WernerVdM97/daily-pixel](https://github.com/WernerVdM97/daily-pixel/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/factory-librarian.md))
- **factory-map**: A map for lookups one grep can't answer. Read it before opening `factory/land.go` (about 1000 lines): find the file and function you need, then read only that part. `go doc` doesn't show unexported methods, so grep `^func (l \*lander) &lt;name&gt;`. ([tomphp/code-central](https://github.com/tomphp/code-central/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/factory-map.md))
- **factory-merge-reviewer**: Cold, read-only reviewer for one pull request. Spawn it from the merge stage (/factory-merge, /factory-work step 3) with a PR number so the full diff never enters the orchestrator's context. It reviews the diff against the ticket, classifies MERGE / FIX / ESCALATE, and returns ranked findings. It never merges, never edits the branch, and never runs the fix loop — the caller acts on the verdict. ([watt-mind/factory](https://github.com/watt-mind/factory/blob/HEAD/dist/pi/agents/factory-merge-reviewer.md) · ★20 · 6 copies)
- **factory-milestone-reviewer**: Read-only, fresh-context reviewer for one factory milestone. Checks the diff against the agent-plan entry (plan conformance), the AC ids it claims (coverage), named regression areas, and the blast-radius boundary from the human plan. Returns VERDICT: PASS or FIX with findings. Spawned by factory Phase 4; usable standalone. NOT a fixer. ([oleg-koval/factory](https://github.com/oleg-koval/factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-milestone-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **factory-muscle-memory**: Dark Factory procedural-memory loop. The other half of the factory's sleep - REM prunes what is no longer true, muscle-memory encodes what keeps being relearned. Mines this repo's session transcripts (interactive and factory, equally) for task families that recur and are hard to follow - red herrings, gotchas, repeated re-derivations - and turns them into one-line notes appended to the reference topic where a reader would look, gated on repetition evidence. When a task family's notes accumulate into a procedure, it files an Inbox issue proposing a skill. Never creates the skill itself. ([WernerVdM97/daily-pixel](https://github.com/WernerVdM97/daily-pixel/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/factory-muscle-memory.md))

- **factory-plan-critic**: Read-only critic of a plan before it is approved (increment_plan.md, design.md): does the plan cover every scenario and contract, is every increment deployable and under the ceiling, is every test case reachable, does it invent business facts, does every open premise hold at a source you can read (a repository file or a contract, cited file:line) — else 🟡 `❓ unverified`; an external premise (library, service, provider) is always `❓ unverified — external`, for the main session to route to the docs reader; you spawn nothing. ([DougZeThug/717rec](https://github.com/DougZeThug/717rec/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-reviewer.md))
- **factory-reviewer**: Factory Independent Reviewer. Challenges plans before substantial work and reviews finished diffs for correctness, regressions, security, over-engineering and whether the original request is actually satisfied. Read-only. Never reviews its own or the builder's work as a favour: findings need evidence. Run it before any `git push` is considered done (a PreToolUse hook reminds you), and on request to review a diff for this class of issue. - **fashion**: Use for fashion design, tailoring, textile craft, cultural dress, and the business of clothing. Triggers on fashion, clothes, clothing, garment, outfit, dress, fabric, textile, tailoring, sewing, pattern, collection, lookbook, style, wardrobe, apparel, streetwear, bridal, Dhaka, Pashmina, daura suruwal. Grounds culture in origin and meaning, never decoration. ([bishrant-personal/orive-creative-studio](https://github.com/bishrant-personal/orive-creative-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/fashion.md))
